    1: import { ReactNode, useCallback } from 'react';
    2: import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from './ui/card';
    3: import { Button } from './ui/button';
    4: import { Badge } from './ui/badge';
    5: import { Crown, Lock, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
    6: import { usePremiumAccess, PremiumFeatureKey, PREMIUM_FEATURES } from '../utils/premium';
    7: 
    8: interface PremiumGateProps {
    9:  featureKey: PremiumFeatureKey;
   10:  children: ReactNode;
   11:  onUpgrade: () => void;
   12:  fallbackTitle?: string;
   13:  fallbackDescription?: string;
   14:  showFullPage?: boolean;
   15:  className?: string;
   16: }
   17: 
   18: /**
   19:  * PremiumGate component that wraps premium features
   20:  * Shows upgrade prompt for non-premium users
   21:  */
   22: export function PremiumGate({
   23:  featureKey,
   24:  children,
   25:  onUpgrade,
   26:  fallbackTitle,
   27:  fallbackDescription,
   28:  showFullPage = false,
   29:  className = ""
   30: }: PremiumGateProps) {
   31:  // Call hooks at top level
   32:  const { checkAccess, getFeatureInfo, getSubscription } = usePremiumAccess();
   33:  
   34:  // Simple access check
   35:  const hasAccess = checkAccess(featureKey);
   36:  const feature = getFeatureInfo(featureKey);
   37:  const subscription = getSubscription();
   38: 
   39:  // If user has access, render the children
   40:  if (hasAccess) {
   41:  return <>{children}</>;
   42:  }
   43: 
   44:  // Show upgrade prompt
   45:  const title = fallbackTitle || feature.name;
   46:  const description = fallbackDescription || feature.description;
   47: 
   48:  if (showFullPage) {
   49:  return (
   50:  <div className={`min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center p-6 ${className}`}>
   51:  <div className="max-w-2xl w-full">
   52:  <Card className="border-2 border-primary/20 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5 to-purple-50">
   53:  <CardHeader className="text-center pb-6">
   54:  <div className="mx-auto mb-4 w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/10 to-purple-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
   55:  <Crown className="w-10 h-10 text-primary" />
   56:  </div>
   57:  <CardTitle className="text-3xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">
   58:  {title}
   59:  </CardTitle>
   60:  <p className="text-lg text-muted-foreground max-w-lg mx-auto">
   61:  {description}
   62:  </p>
   63:  </CardHeader>
   64:  <CardContent className="text-center space-y-6">
   65:  <div className="bg-white rounded-lg p-6 border border-border">
   66:  <h4 className="font-semibold text-foreground mb-3">This premium feature includes:</h4>
   67:  <ul className="text-sm text-muted-foreground space-y-2 mb-6">
   68:  <li className="flex items-center">
   69:  <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-primary rounded-full mr-3"></div>
   70:  Advanced AI-powered functionality
   71:  </li>
   72:  <li className="flex items-center">
   73:  <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-primary rounded-full mr-3"></div>
   74:  Priority customer support
   75:  </li>
   76:  <li className="flex items-center">
   77:  <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-primary rounded-full mr-3"></div>
   78:  Enhanced analytics and insights
   79:  </li>
   80:  <li className="flex items-center">
   81:  <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-primary rounded-full mr-3"></div>
   82:  Regular feature updates
   83:  </li>
   84:  </ul>
   85: 
   86:  <div className="space-y-3">
   87:  <Button 
   88:  onClick={onUpgrade}
   89:  size="lg"
   90:  className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-primary to-primary-hover hover:from-primary-hover hover:to-primary text-white font-semibold py-3"
   91:  >
   92:  <Crown className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
   93:  View Pro Access
   94:  <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 ml-2" />
   95:  </Button>
   96:  
   97:  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   98:  Pro access is currently enabled manually while online payments are being prepared.
   99:  </p>
  100:  </div>
  101:  </div>
  102: 
  103:  {subscription.tier === 'free' && (
  104:  <div className="text-left bg-muted/30 rounded-lg p-4">
  105:  <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
  106:  <Lock className="w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
  107:  <div>
  108:  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-1">
  109:  Unlock Premium Features
  110:  </p>
  111:  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
  112:  Get access to advanced AI tools, priority support, and exclusive features 
  113:  designed to accelerate your hiring process.
  114:  </p>
  115:  </div>
  116:  </div>
  117:  </div>
  118:  )}
  119:  </CardContent>
  120:  </Card>
  121:  </div>
  122:  </div>
  123:  );
  124:  }
  125: 
  126:  // Compact version for inline use
  127:  return (
  128:  <Card className={`border-2 border-primary/20 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5 to-purple-50/30 ${className}`}>
  129:  <CardContent className="p-6 text-center">
  130:  <div className="mb-4">
  131:  <div className="mx-auto mb-3 w-12 h-12 bg-primary/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
  132:  <Crown className="w-6 h-6 text-primary" />
  133:  </div>
  134:  <h3 className="font-semibold text-foreground mb-2">{title}</h3>
  135:  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4">{description}</p>
  136:  </div>
  137:  
  138:  <Button 
  139:  onClick={onUpgrade}
  140:  className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-primary to-primary-hover hover:from-primary-hover hover:to-primary text-white font-medium"
  141:  >
  142:  <Crown className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
  143:  View Pro Access
  144:  </Button>
  145:  
  146:  </CardContent>
  147:  </Card>
  148:  );
  149: }
  150: 
  151: /**
  152:  * Utility component for premium feature badges
  153:  */
  154: export function PremiumBadge({ 
  155:  featureKey, 
  156:  className = "" 
  157: }: { 
  158:  featureKey: PremiumFeatureKey;
  159:  className?: string;
  160: }) {
  161:  const { checkAccess } = usePremiumAccess();
  162:  const hasAccess = checkAccess(featureKey);
  163: 
  164:  if (hasAccess) return null;
  165: 
  166:  return (
  167:  <Badge className={`bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs ${className}`}>
  168:  <Crown className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
  169:  Pro
  170:  </Badge>
  171:  );
  172: }
  173: 
  174: /**
  175:  * Hook for premium feature navigation with automatic access checking
  176:  */
  177: export function usePremiumNavigation(onUpgrade: () => void) {
  178:  const { checkAccess } = usePremiumAccess();
  179: 
  180:  const navigateToFeature = useCallback((
  181:  featureKey: PremiumFeatureKey,
  182:  navigationCallback: () => void,
  183:  fallbackMessage?: string
  184:  ) => {
  185:  try {
  186:  if (checkAccess(featureKey)) {
  187:  navigationCallback();
  188:  } else {
  189:  const feature = PREMIUM_FEATURES[featureKey];
  190:  console.log(`Premium access required for: ${feature.name}`);
  191:  onUpgrade();
  192:  }
  193:  } catch (error) {
  194:  console.error('Error in premium navigation:', error);
  195:  onUpgrade(); // Fallback to upgrade page
  196:  }
  197:  }, [checkAccess, onUpgrade]);
  198: 
  199:  return { 
  200:  navigateToFeature, 
  201:  checkAccess: useCallback((featureKey: PremiumFeatureKey) => {
  202:  try {
  203:  return checkAccess(featureKey);
  204:  } catch (error) {
  205:  console.error('Error checking premium access:', error);
  206:  return false;
  207:  }
  208:  }, [checkAccess])
  209:  };
  210: }

============================================================
src\hirevify-app\components\AuthProvider.tsx
============================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:  createContext,
    5:  useCallback,
    6:  useContext,
    7:  useEffect,
    8:  useState,
    9:  ReactNode,
   10: } from "react";
   11: import { toast } from "sonner";
   12: import { supabase } from "@/src/lib/supabase";
   13: import type { Session } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   14: 
   15: export interface User {
   16:  id: string;
   17:  email: string;
   18:  name: string;
   19:  userType: "recruiter" | "candidate";
   20:  isEmailVerified: boolean;
   21:  profileComplete: boolean;
   22:  createdAt: string;
   23:  accessToken: string;
   24: }
   25: 
   26: interface AuthContextType {
   27:  user: User | null;
   28:  accessToken: string | null;
   29:  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string | null>;
   30:  setUser: (user: User | null) => void;
   31:  isLoading: boolean;
   32:  authInitialized: boolean;
   33:  connectionStatus: "checking" | "connected" | "error";
   34:  signUp: (
   35:  email: string,
   36:  password: string,
   37:  name: string,
   38:  userType: "recruiter" | "candidate",
   39:  companyName?: string
   40:  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; user?: User }>;
   41:  signIn: (
   42:  email: string,
   43:  password: string
   44:  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; user?: User }>;
   45:  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
   46: }
   47: 
   48: const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);
   49: 
   50: interface AuthProviderProps {
   51:  children: ReactNode;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function mapUserTypeToDbRole(userType: "recruiter" | "candidate") {
   55:  return userType;
   56: }
   57: 
   58: function mapDbRoleToUserType(role: string): "recruiter" | "candidate" {
   59:  return role === "recruiter"? "recruiter": "candidate";
   60: }
   61: export function AuthProvider({ children }: AuthProviderProps) {
   62:  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
   63:  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
   64:  const [authInitialized, setAuthInitialized] = useState(false);
   65:  const [connectionStatus, setConnectionStatus] =
   66:  useState<"checking" | "connected" | "error">("checking");
   67: 
   68:  const setUserWithTokenSync = useCallback((newUser: User | null) => {
   69:  setUser(newUser);
   70: 
   71:  if (newUser) {
   72:  localStorage.setItem("hirevify_user", JSON.stringify(newUser));
   73:  localStorage.setItem("hirevify_access_token", newUser.accessToken || "");
   74:  } else {
   75:  localStorage.removeItem("hirevify_user");
   76:  localStorage.removeItem("hirevify_access_token");
   77:  }
   78:  }, []);
   79: 
   80:  const loadUserFromSession = useCallback(async (session: Session) => {
   81:  try {
   82:  const authUser = session.user;
   83: 
   84:  const { data: profile, error } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("auth_user_id", authUser.id).maybeSingle();
   85: 
   86:  if (error) {
   87:  console.warn("Profile load failed:", error.message);
   88:  }
   89: 
   90:  const loadedUser: User = {
   91:  id: profile?.id || authUser.id,
   92:  email: authUser.email || "",
   93:  name: profile?.full_name || authUser.user_metadata?.name || "HireVify User",
   94:  userType: mapDbRoleToUserType(profile?.role || authUser.user_metadata?.userType || "employee"),
   95:  isEmailVerified:!!authUser.email_confirmed_at,
   96:  profileComplete: true,
   97:  createdAt: profile?.created_at || authUser.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   98:  accessToken: session.access_token || "",
   99:  };
  100: 
  101:  setUserWithTokenSync(loadedUser);
  102:  } catch (error) {
  103:  console.error("Failed to load user from session:", error);
  104:  }
  105:  }, [setUserWithTokenSync]);
  106: 
  107:  const initializeAuth = useCallback(async () => {
  108:  try {
  109:  setIsLoading(true);
  110:  setConnectionStatus("checking");
  111: 
  112:  const { error } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("id").limit(1);
  113: 
  114:  if (error) {
  115:  console.warn("Supabase connection check failed:", error.message);
  116:  setConnectionStatus("error");
  117:  } else {
  118:  setConnectionStatus("connected");
  119:  }
  120: 
  121:  const { data: sessionData } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
  122: 
  123:  if (sessionData.session?.user) {
  124:  await loadUserFromSession(sessionData.session);
  125:  }
  126:  } catch (error) {
  127:  console.error("Auth initialization failed:", error);
  128:  setConnectionStatus("error");
  129:  } finally {
  130:  setIsLoading(false);
  131:  setAuthInitialized(true);
  132:  }
  133:  }, [loadUserFromSession]);
  134: 
  135:  useEffect(() => {
  136:  let active = true;
  137: 
  138:  queueMicrotask(() => {
  139:  if (active) {
  140:  void initializeAuth();
  141:  }
  142:  });
  143: 
  144:  const {
  145:  data: { subscription },
  146:  } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
  147:  if (!session?.user) {
  148:  setUserWithTokenSync(null);
  149:  return;
  150:  }
  151: 
  152:  await loadUserFromSession(session);
  153:  });
  154: 
  155:  return () => {
  156:  active = false;
  157:  subscription.unsubscribe();
  158:  };
  159:  }, [initializeAuth, loadUserFromSession, setUserWithTokenSync]);
  160: 
  161:  const signUp = async (
  162:  email: string,
  163:  password: string,
  164:  name: string,
  165:  userType: "recruiter" | "candidate",
  166:  companyName?: string
  167:  ) => {
  168:  try {
  169:  setIsLoading(true);
  170: 
  171:  const cleanEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
  172:  const cleanName = name.trim();
  173:  const cleanCompanyName = userType === "recruiter"? (companyName || "").trim(): "";
  174: 
  175:  if (!cleanEmail ||!password ||!cleanName ||!userType) {
  176:  throw new Error("All fields are required");
  177:  }
  178: 
  179:  if (userType === "recruiter" &&!cleanCompanyName) {
  180:  throw new Error("Company name is required for recruiter accounts");
  181:  }
  182: 
  183:  if (password.length < 8) {
  184:  throw new Error("Password must be at least 8 characters long");
  185:  }
  186: 
  187:  const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
  188:  if (!emailRegex.test(cleanEmail)) {
  189:  throw new Error("Please enter a valid email address");
  190:  }
  191: 
  192:  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
  193:  email: cleanEmail,
  194:  password,
  195:  options: {
  196:  data: {
  197:  name: cleanName,
  198:  userType,
  199:  companyName: cleanCompanyName || undefined,
  200:  },
  201:  },
  202:  });
  203: 
  204:  if (error) {
  205:  throw new Error(error.message);
  206:  }
  207: 
  208:  if (!data.user) {
  209:  throw new Error("Signup failed. Please try again.");
  210:  }
  211: 
  212:  const dbRole = mapUserTypeToDbRole(userType);
  213: 
  214: const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase.from("profiles").upsert(
  215:  {
  216:  auth_user_id: data.user.id,
  217:  full_name: cleanName,
  218:  email: cleanEmail,
  219:  role: dbRole,
  220:  company_name: userType === "recruiter"? cleanCompanyName: null,
  221:  },
  222:  {
  223:  onConflict: "auth_user_id",
  224:  }
  225:  ).select("*").single();
  226: 
  227: if (profileError) {
  228:  console.error("Profile insert failed:", profileError);
  229:  const errorMessage = typeof profileError === 'object' && profileError!== null && 'message' in profileError? (profileError as any).message: String(profileError);
  230:  throw new Error(`Profile creation failed: ${errorMessage}`);
  231: }
  232: 
  233: if (userType === "recruiter" && cleanCompanyName && profile?.id) {
  234:  const { error: recruiterProfileError } = await supabase.from("recruiter_profiles").upsert(
  235:  {
  236:  id: profile.id,
  237:  user_id: profile.id,
  238:  company_name: cleanCompanyName,
  239:  },
  240:  {
  241:  onConflict: "id",
  242:  }
  243:  );
  244: 
  245:  if (recruiterProfileError) {
  246:  console.warn("Recruiter profile bootstrap failed:", recruiterProfileError.message);
  247:  }
  248: }
  249: 
  250:  const session = data.session;
  251: 
  252:  const newUser: User = {
  253:  id: profile?.id || data.user.id,
  254:  email: cleanEmail,
  255:  name: cleanName,
  256:  userType,
  257:  isEmailVerified:!!data.user.email_confirmed_at,
  258:  profileComplete: true,
  259:  createdAt: profile?.created_at || data.user.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
  260:  accessToken: session?.access_token || "",
  261:  };
  262: 
  263:  if (session?.access_token) {
  264:  setUserWithTokenSync(newUser);
  265:  }
  266: 
  267:  return {
  268:  success: true,
  269:  message: session? "Account created successfully! Welcome to HireVify.": "Account created. Please check your email to confirm your account.",
  270:  user: newUser,
  271:  };
  272:  } catch (error) {
  273:  console.error("Signup error:", error);
  274:  return {
  275:  success: false,
  276:  message: error instanceof Error? error.message: "Signup failed",
  277:  };
  278:  } finally {
  279:  setIsLoading(false);
  280:  }
  281:  };
  282: 
  283:  const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
  284:  try {
  285:  setIsLoading(true);
  286: 
  287:  const cleanEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
  288: 
  289:  if (!cleanEmail ||!password) {
  290:  throw new Error("Email and password are required");
  291:  }
  292: 
  293:  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
  294:  email: cleanEmail,
  295:  password,
  296:  });
  297: 
  298:  if (error) {
  299:  throw new Error(error.message);
  300:  }
  301: 
  302:  if (!data.session ||!data.user) {
  303:  throw new Error("Login failed. Please try again.");
  304:  }
  305: 
  306:  await loadUserFromSession(data.session);
  307: 
  308:  const storedUser = localStorage.getItem("hirevify_user");
  309:  const loggedInUser = storedUser? (JSON.parse(storedUser) as User): undefined;
  310: 
  311:  return {
  312:  success: true,
  313:  message: "Successfully signed in! Welcome back.",
  314:  user: loggedInUser,
  315:  };
  316:  } catch (error) {
  317:  console.error("Signin error:", error);
  318:  return {
  319:  success: false,
  320:  message: error instanceof Error? error.message: "Sign in failed. Please try again.",
  321:  };
  322:  } finally {
  323:  setIsLoading(false);
  324:  }
  325:  };
  326: 
  327:  const signOut = async () => {
  328:  try {
  329:  await supabase.auth.signOut();
  330:  setUserWithTokenSync(null);
  331:  } catch (error) {
  332:  console.error("Sign out error:", error);
  333:  setUserWithTokenSync(null);
  334:  toast.error("Error signing out, but session cleared");
  335:  }
  336:  };
  337: 
  338:  const accessToken = user?.accessToken || null;
  339: 
  340:  const getAccessToken = useCallback(async (): Promise<string | null> => {
  341:  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
  342: 
  343:  if (error) {
  344:  console.error("Failed to get access token:", error.message);
  345:  return user?.accessToken || null;
  346:  }
  347: 
  348:  return data.session?.access_token || user?.accessToken || null;
  349:  }, [user?.accessToken]);
  350:  return (
  351:  <AuthContext.Provider
  352:  value={{
  353:  user,
  354:  setUser: setUserWithTokenSync,
  355:  accessToken,
  356:  getAccessToken,
  357:  isLoading,
  358:  authInitialized,
  359:  connectionStatus,
  360:  signUp,
  361:  signIn,
  362:  signOut,
  363:  }}
  364:  >
  365:  {children}
  366:  </AuthContext.Provider>
  367:  );
  368: }
  369: 
  370: export function useAuth() {
  371:  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  372: 
  373:  if (context === undefined) {
  374:  throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  375:  }
  376: 
  377:  return context;
  378: }
  379:

src\hirevify-app\components\BillingSettingsCard.tsx
============================================================

    1: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Ban, CreditCard, PauseCircle, PlayCircle } from 'lucide-react';
    3: import { Button } from './ui/button';
    4: import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from './ui/card';
    5: import { Badge } from './ui/badge';
    6: import { Label } from './ui/label';
    7: import { Separator } from './ui/separator';
    8: import { toast } from 'sonner';
    9: import { subscriptionsService, type Subscription } from '@/src/hirevify-app/services/subscriptionsService';
   10: 
   11: type BillingSettingsCardProps = {
   12:   userId: string | null;
   13:   userEmail?: string;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: function formatDate(value?: string | null) {
   17:   if (!value) return 'No expiry';
   18:   return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString();
   19: }
   20: 
   21: function daysRemaining(value?: string | null) {
   22:   if (!value) return null;
   23:   return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(value).getTime() - Date.now()) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)));
   24: }
   25: 
   26: export function BillingSettingsCard({ userId, userEmail }: BillingSettingsCardProps) {
   27:   const [subscription, setSubscription] = useState<Subscription | null>(null);
   28:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   29:   const [action, setAction] = useState<'freeze' | 'unfreeze' | 'cancel' | null>(null);
   30: 
   31:   const loadSubscription = useCallback(async () => {
   32:     if (!userId) {
   33:       setSubscription(null);
   34:       setLoading(false);
   35:       return;
   36:     }
   37: 
   38:     setLoading(true);
   39:     const { data } = await subscriptionsService.getUserSubscription(userId);
   40:     setSubscription(data);
   41:     setLoading(false);
   42:   }, [userId]);
   43: 
   44:   useEffect(() => {
   45:     void loadSubscription();
   46:   }, [loadSubscription]);
   47: 
   48:   const remainingDays = useMemo(() => {
   49:     if (!subscription) return null;
   50:     if (subscription.status === 'frozen') return Number(subscription.frozen_remaining_days || 0);
   51:     return daysRemaining(subscription.expires_at);
   52:   }, [subscription]);
   53: 
   54:   const canFreeze = Boolean(
   55:     subscription &&
   56:       subscription.tier !== 'free' &&
   57:       !subscription.freeze_used &&
   58:       subscription.status !== 'frozen' &&
   59:       subscription.status !== 'canceled' &&
   60:       subscription.status !== 'expired',
   61:   );
   62:   const canUnfreeze = subscription?.status === 'frozen';
   63:   const canCancel = Boolean(subscription && subscription.tier !== 'free' && subscription.status !== 'canceled' && subscription.status !== 'expired');
   64: 
   65:   const runAction = async (nextAction: 'freeze' | 'unfreeze' | 'cancel') => {
   66:     if (!userId) {
   67:       toast.error('Subscription profile is still loading.');
   68:       return;
   69:     }
   70: 
   71:     setAction(nextAction);
   72:     try {
   73:       const result =
   74:         nextAction === 'freeze'
   75:           ? await subscriptionsService.freezeSubscription(userId)
   76:           : nextAction === 'unfreeze'
   77:             ? await subscriptionsService.unfreezeSubscription(userId)
   78:             : await subscriptionsService.cancelSubscription(userId);
   79: 
   80:       if (result.error) {
   81:         throw result.error;
   82:       }
   83: 
   84:       toast.success(
   85:         nextAction === 'freeze'
   86:           ? 'Subscription frozen. Pro access is paused.'
   87:           : nextAction === 'unfreeze'
   88:             ? 'Subscription unfrozen. Remaining days restarted.'
   89:             : 'Subscription canceled. Access continues until expiry.',
   90:       );
   91:       await loadSubscription();
   92:     } catch (error) {
   93:       toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Billing action failed.');
   94:     } finally {
   95:       setAction(null);
   96:     }
   97:   };
   98: 
   99:   return (
  100:     <Card>
  101:       <CardHeader>
  102:         <CardTitle className="flex items-center">
  103:           <CreditCard className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600" />
  104:           Billing
  105:         </CardTitle>
  106:       </CardHeader>
  107:       <CardContent className="space-y-5">
  108:         <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  109:           <div>
  110:             <Label className="text-slate-500">Plan</Label>
  111:             <div className="mt-2">
  112:               <Badge className="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700">
  113:                 {loading ? 'Loading' : `${subscription?.tier || 'free'} / ${subscription?.status || 'active'}`}
  114:               </Badge>
  115:             </div>
  116:           </div>
  117:           <div>
  118:             <Label className="text-slate-500">Expires</Label>
  119:             <p className="mt-2 font-medium text-slate-950">{formatDate(subscription?.expires_at)}</p>
  120:           </div>
  121:           <div>
  122:             <Label className="text-slate-500">Days left</Label>
  123:             <p className="mt-2 font-medium text-slate-950">{remainingDays === null ? 'Not applicable' : `${remainingDays} days`}</p>
  124:           </div>
  125:         </div>
  126: 
  127:         <Separator />
  128: 
  129:         <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  130:           Yearly plans can be frozen once. While frozen, Pro features become Free. Remaining days stop counting and restart when the plan is unfrozen.
  131:           Canceled subscriptions cannot be frozen, but paid access continues until the current expiry date.
  132:         </div>
  133: 
  134:         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  135:           {canFreeze && (
  136:             <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => void runAction('freeze')} disabled={Boolean(action)}>
  137:               <PauseCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
  138:               {action === 'freeze' ? 'Freezing...' : 'Freeze subscription'}
  139:             </Button>
  140:           )}
  141:           {canUnfreeze && (
  142:             <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => void runAction('unfreeze')} disabled={Boolean(action)}>
  143:               <PlayCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
  144:               {action === 'unfreeze' ? 'Unfreezing...' : 'Unfreeze subscription'}
  145:             </Button>
  146:           )}
  147:           {canCancel && (
  148:             <Button variant="outline" className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700" onClick={() => void runAction('cancel')} disabled={Boolean(action)}>
  149:               <Ban className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
  150:               {action === 'cancel' ? 'Canceling...' : 'Cancel subscription'}
  151:             </Button>
  152:           )}
  153:         </div>
  154: 
  155:         {subscription?.freeze_used && subscription.status !== 'frozen' && (
  156:           <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">Freeze has already been used for this subscription.</p>
  157:         )}
  158:         <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Billing account: {userEmail || 'Not available'}</p>
  159:       </CardContent>
  160:     </Card>
  161:   );
  162: }

src\hirevify-app\services\subscriptionsService.ts
============================================================

    1: /**
    2:  * Subscriptions Service
    3:  * Handles subscription and premium access from Supabase
    4:  */
    5: 
    6: import { createSupabaseBrowserClient } from '@/src/lib/supabase';
    7: 
    8: export interface Subscription {
    9:  id: string;
   10:  user_id: string;
   11:  tier: 'free' | 'pro';
   12:  status: 'active' | 'past_due' | 'canceled' | 'expired' | 'frozen';
   13:  stripe_subscription_id: string | null;
   14:  stripe_customer_id: string | null;
   15:  started_at: string;
   16:  expires_at: string | null;
   17:  trial_ends_at: string | null;
   18:  auto_renew: boolean;
   19:  freeze_used?: boolean | null;
   20:  frozen_at?: string | null;
   21:  frozen_remaining_days?: number | null;
   22:   created_at: string;
   23:  updated_at: string;
   24:  isActive: boolean;
   25: }
   26: 
   27: class SubscriptionsService {
   28:  private supabase = createSupabaseBrowserClient();
   29: 
   30:  private freeSubscription(userId: string): Subscription {
   31:  return {
   32:  id: userId,
   33:  user_id: userId,
   34:  tier: 'free',
   35:  status: 'active',
   36:  stripe_subscription_id: null,
   37:  stripe_customer_id: null,
   38:  started_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   39:  expires_at: null,
   40:  trial_ends_at: null,
   41:  auto_renew: false,
   42:  created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   43:  updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   44:  isActive: false,
   45:  };
   46:  }
   47: 
   48:  private withAccessState(subscription: Subscription): Subscription {
   49:  const expiresAt = subscription.expires_at ? new Date(subscription.expires_at).getTime() : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   50:  return {
   51:  ...subscription,
   52:  isActive: subscription.tier === 'pro' && subscription.status === 'active' && expiresAt > Date.now(),
   53:  };
   54:  }
   55: 
   56:  /**
   57:  * Get subscription for a user
   58:  */
   59:  async getUserSubscription(userId: string) {
   60:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').select('*').eq('user_id', userId).maybeSingle<Subscription>();
   61: 
   62:  if (error) {
   63:  return { data: this.freeSubscription(userId), error: null };
   64:  }
   65: 
   66:  return { data: data ? this.withAccessState(data) : this.freeSubscription(userId), error: null };
   67:  }
   68: 
   69:  /**
   70:  * Create or update subscription
   71:  */
   72:  async upsertSubscription(userId: string, subscription: Partial<Omit<Subscription, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'>>) {
   73:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').upsert([{...subscription, user_id: userId }], {
   74:  onConflict: 'user_id',
   75:  }).select().single<Subscription>();
   76: 
   77:  if (error) {
   78:  console.error('Error upserting subscription:', error);
   79:  return { data: null, error };
   80:  }
   81: 
   82:  return { data, error: null };
   83:  }
   84: 
   85:  /**
   86:  * Check if user has active premium subscription
   87:  */
   88:  async hasPremiumAccess(userId: string) {
   89:  const { data, error } = await this.getUserSubscription(userId);
   90: 
   91:  if (error) {
   92:  return { hasPremium: false, error };
   93:  }
   94: 
   95:  const now = new Date();
   96:  const isActive = data && data.tier === 'pro' && data.status === 'active' && (!data.expires_at || new Date(data.expires_at) > now);
   97: 
   98:  return { hasPremium:!!isActive, subscription: data, error: null };
   99:  }
  100: 
  101:  /**
  102:  * Check if user is in trial period
  103:  */
  104:  async isInTrial(userId: string) {
  105:  const { data, error } = await this.getUserSubscription(userId);
  106: 
  107:  if (error ||!data) {
  108:  return { inTrial: false, error };
  109:  }
  110: 
  111:  const now = new Date();
  112:  const inTrial =
  113:  data.tier === 'pro' &&
  114:  data.trial_ends_at &&
  115:  new Date(data.trial_ends_at) > now;
  116: 
  117:  return { inTrial:!!inTrial, trialEndsAt: data.trial_ends_at, error: null };
  118:  }
  119: 
  120:  /**
  121:  * Cancel subscription
  122:  */
  123:  async cancelSubscription(userId: string) {
  124:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').update({
  125:  status: 'canceled',
  126:  auto_renew: false,
  127:  }).eq('user_id', userId).select().single<Subscription>();
  128: 
  129:  if (error) {
  130:  console.error('Error canceling subscription:', error);
  131:  return { data: null, error };
  132:  }
  133: 
  134:  return { data, error: null };
  135:  }
  136: 
  137:  async freezeSubscription(userId: string) {
  138:  const { data: subscription } = await this.getUserSubscription(userId);
  139: 
  140:  if (!subscription || subscription.tier === 'free') {
  141:  return { data: null, error: new Error('Only active paid subscriptions can be frozen.') };
  142:  }
  143: 
  144:  if (subscription.status === 'canceled') {
  145:  return { data: null, error: new Error('Canceled subscriptions cannot be frozen.') };
  146:  }
  147: 
  148:  if (subscription.status === 'frozen') {
  149:  return { data: subscription, error: null };
  150:  }
  151: 
  152:  if (subscription.freeze_used) {
  153:  return { data: null, error: new Error('This subscription has already used its one freeze.') };
  154:  }
  155: 
  156:  const expiresAt = subscription.expires_at ? new Date(subscription.expires_at).getTime() : Date.now() + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  157:  const remainingDays = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((expiresAt - Date.now()) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)));
  158: 
  159:  if (remainingDays <= 0) {
  160:  return { data: null, error: new Error('This subscription has no remaining days to freeze.') };
  161:  }
  162: 
  163:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').update({
  164:  status: 'frozen',
  165:  auto_renew: false,
  166:  freeze_used: true,
  167:  frozen_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  168:  frozen_remaining_days: remainingDays,
  169:  }).eq('user_id', userId).select().single<Subscription>();
  170: 
  171:  if (error) {
  172:  return { data: null, error };
  173:  }
  174: 
  175:  return { data, error: null };
  176:  }
  177: 
  178:  async unfreezeSubscription(userId: string) {
  179:  const { data: subscription } = await this.getUserSubscription(userId);
  180: 
  181:  if (!subscription || subscription.status !== 'frozen') {
  182:  return { data: null, error: new Error('Only frozen subscriptions can be unfrozen.') };
  183:  }
  184: 
  185:  const remainingDays = Math.max(1, Number(subscription.frozen_remaining_days || 0));
  186:  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + remainingDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  187: 
  188:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').update({
  189:  status: 'active',
  190:  expires_at: expiresAt,
  191:  frozen_at: null,
  192:  frozen_remaining_days: null,
  193:  }).eq('user_id', userId).select().single<Subscription>();
  194: 
  195:  if (error) {
  196:  return { data: null, error };
  197:  }
  198: 
  199:  return { data, error: null };
  200:  }
  201: 
  202:  /**
  203:  * Upgrade subscription tier
  204:  */
  205:  async upgradeSubscription(userId: string, newTier: 'pro') {
  206:  const expiresAt = new Date();
  207:  expiresAt.setFullYear(expiresAt.getFullYear() + 1);
  208: 
  209:  const { data, error } = await this.supabase.from('subscriptions').update({
  210:  tier: newTier,
  211:  status: 'active',
  212:  expires_at: expiresAt.toISOString(),
  213:  }).eq('user_id', userId).select().single<Subscription>();
  214: 
  215:  if (error) {
  216:  console.error('Error upgrading subscription:', error);
  217:  return { data: null, error };
  218:  }
  219: 
  220:  return { data, error: null };
  221:  }
  222: 
  223:  /**
  224:  * Get subscription tier name
  225:  */
  226:  getTierName(tier: 'free' | 'pro') {
  227:  const tierNames = {
  228:  free: 'Free',
  229:  pro: 'Pro',
  230:  };
  231:  return tierNames[tier];
  232:  }
  233: 
  234:  /**
  235:  * Get features for tier
  236:  */
  237:  getTierFeatures(tier: 'free' | 'pro') {
  238:  const features = {
  239:  free: [
  240:  'Post up to 5 jobs/month',
  241:  'Apply to 5 projects/month',
  242:  'Basic profile',
  243:  'Limited job search',
  244:  ],
  245:  pro: [
  246:  'Unlimited job postings',
  247:  'Unlimited applications',
  248:  'Advanced profile',
  249:  'AI-powered matching',
  250:  'Video interviews',
  251:  'Custom assessments',
  252:  'Analytics & insights',
  253:  'Priority support',
  254:  ],
  255:  };
  256:  return features[tier];
  257:  }
  258: }
  259: 
  260: export const subscriptionsService = new SubscriptionsService();
  261: 
  262:
